Scratch scripts `.tmp/a4-uia.mjs` and `.tmp/a4-uia.ps1` (git-ignored, not committed): launch the page headed, find the window by title, and dump the document subtree with control type and name. Not a screen reader: UIA is a platform accessibility API that screen readers such as Narrator use; NVDA reads Firefox through IAccessible2 instead.

| Check | Command | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `examples/components-only`, Firefox | `node .tmp/a4-uia.mjs firefox` | `region "Billing"` (card `label`), `region "Plan"` (named by the slotted heading through `ariaLabelledByElements`), no region for the unnamed card; `edit "Email"` with its value; `button "Reset"`, `"Save"`, and `"Billing help"` (icon-only, from `label`). Each `odd-button` host appears as an unnamed `group` around its button, and `odd-input` adds a `group "Email"` before the field. |
| Same, after a blocked submission (empty required field) | `node .tmp/a4-uia.mjs firefox /examples/components-only/ "Components only" submit` | Same names; value empty. Required, invalid, and the description are **not observable** through this client: `IsRequiredForForm` stays false although the inner `<input>` has `required` and `aria-describedby`, and the managed client does not resolve `FullDescription`/`AriaProperties`. Not counted either way. |
| `examples/side-nav`, Firefox | `node .tmp/a4-uia.mjs firefox /examples/side-nav/ ide` | `navigation "Primary, narrow"` and `"Primary, wide"`, nested lists and list items, links with their URLs, `button "Guides"` (group disclosure), `button "New project"` (action). Observation: a link with a count reads `"Components 4"` (the count joins the name with no context). |
| WebKit (Playwright build for Windows) and Chromium | same script | WebKit exposes no document to UIA on Windows. Chromium builds its tree only when it detects an assistive technology (not forced here; its tree is already covered through the DevTools protocol in `e2e/components-only.spec.ts`). |

**Not done:** WebKit's native tree (needs macOS: Accessibility Inspector or VoiceOver); required/invalid/description in Firefox through IAccessible2 (needs an IA2 client such as NVDA's or Accessibility Insights); screen readers.

**Environment:** Windows, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.64.1 (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit), docs dev server on 127.0.0.1:4177 with its strict CSP (Trusted Types `oddkin-template`, `style-src 'self'`). Panels from Agent 3's 77aaa62. `apps/docs/package.json` declares `@oddkin/workbench` (bf00c05). Other agents had unrelated edits in the worktree. No full-pipeline run.

| Check | Command | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Typecheck | `npx tsc -b apps/docs`; `npx tsc -p tsconfig.tests.json --noEmit` | Pass. |
| Boundaries | `node scripts/architecture/check-boundaries.mjs` | `Boundaries OK` (docs → workbench allowed and declared). |
| Docs e2e | `npx playwright test e2e/docs.spec.ts --repeat-each=3` | 36 passed. The side-nav guide embeds `<oddkin-component-panel>` (open shadow root) with no component module loaded; the components page is `<oddkin-catalog-panel>`: typing `card` gives `/components?q=card`, "Showing 1 of 4 components", focus stays in the search field, clearing restores `/components`, and Back returns to the guide (no entries added); a link inside the panel's shadow root navigates through the docs router to `/components/odd-button`, whose panel shows the define-entry import and "Preview unavailable" and loads no further component module; the spacing foundation panel shows `space.md --odd-space-md`. Component modules loaded by the whole flow: only `odd-card` (catalog cards) and its styles. |
| Mutants | 5 edits to `apps/docs/src/main.ts`, docs rebuilt each time, Chromium | 4 killed: URL mirroring removed, mirroring with a history entry, the catalog re-rendered on filter changes (focus lost), wrong page key. Survived: loading the catalog define entry without awaiting it; equivalent here, because the panels accept properties set before their element is defined. (Removing the load entirely is rejected by the typechecker and is not counted.) |

**Fixed while integrating:** importing every panel eagerly loaded `odd-card` on every page (the catalog's define entry registers it); panels now load per route. The static `@oddkin/workbench/data` import delayed the app's start enough that a Firefox click on a static side-nav link sometimes happened before the router ran; the builder now loads in parallel with the published files, and the test waits for client-rendered content before its first click.

**Finding (engine):** WebKit reports a `style-src-attr` CSP violation for every `<select>` inserted under `style-src 'self'`, including one created with `createElement` on a page without any Oddkin code (Chromium and Firefox report none; repro run 2026-10-21). In the docs they arise from the panels' selects; when the element is already detached the event targets the document. The spec records violations by event and exempts only WebKit `style-src-attr` with a `select` or `#document` target.

## 2026-10-21 — 1,000-cycle lifetime and retained memory (M8 item 917, app/router/tokens/components)

**Environment:** Windows, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.64.1 (Chromium 156.0.8078.4, Firefox 157.0, WebKit 27.2), local 127.0.0.1 server started by the runner; machine shared with other agents' test runs (timings are indicative). Bundled with esbuild from the packages' built `dist`.

| Check | Command | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Lifetime cycles | `node research/benchmarks/lifetime/run.mjs` (warm-up 100, then two batches of 1,000) | Every scenario in every engine: core `scopeStats()` (scopes, listeners, timers, effects) equal before and after each batch; no page errors. App start/stop with router, resource, command, a `resize` listener, and a timer: 1,000 resource loads per batch; navigation round trips: 2,000 commits per batch, all counters 0 after the app stops (skipped in WebKit: it refuses more than 100 history writes per 10 s); theme edits: 357 distinct `--odd-ref-brand-600` values read back from the frame per batch; components: all four rendered in each of 1,000 cycles, none left in the container. |
| Retained heap (Chromium) | same run; CDP `HeapProfiler.collectGarbage` + `Runtime.getHeapUsage` before and after each batch | Bytes per cycle, first / second batch: app 131 / 3; navigation 11 / 0; theme edits 65 / −9; components 317 / 38. Five further batches of 1,000 component cycles: heap 2.03–2.36 MB, oscillating without growth; DOM listener count back to 13 each time. |
| Batch timings | WebKit and Firefox, four batches of 300 component cycles | Flat (WebKit 14.9–16.5 s, Firefox 2.1–3.1 s); an earlier slower second batch did not recur and was run-to-run noise. |
| Harness sensitivity | 100 cycles with an injected leak, then reverted | `createScope()` without dispose per cycle: "ownership LEAKED" in Chromium and Firefox; 10 KB array kept per cycle: 10,042 / 10,064 B/cycle in Chromium (heap is only measured in Chromium). |

**Fixed in the harness while measuring:** the first navigation scenario compared a batch ending on a two-level route with a sample on a one-level route (a +1 scope that was route structure, not a leak); cycles are now round trips. An upgrade check read the shadow root before connection (Oddkin elements attach it on connect). The component scenario left its container on the page.

**Not covered:** Workbench preview switching and Theme Studio UI edits (Agent 3's surfaces); retained heap in Firefox and WebKit (no comparable API).

**Change:** `@oddkin/core/template/roles` exports `rolesOf(host)` (frozen tag references for the host's registered definition's `dependencies`, cached per definition), `composite` (`html` that accepts a tag reference in tag position, validates the name, and writes it into strings cached per call site and names), and `tag(definition)`. Core's template parser and element base class are unchanged; plain `html`'s tag-position error now points to this module. The analyzer reads `composite` templates like `html` ones, including parts on role elements. ADR 0043 is Accepted with the implemented design; core-contracts §6.3 (contract `core/roles`) and multi-product.md §8 record it.

**Remaining:** the role compatibility check (ADR 0043 point 5), with the first M10 composite.

## 2026-10-22 — Workbench live-update spec: shell reload in WebKit (CI run 6)

**Environment:** Windows, Node 24.22.1, Playwright 1.64.1 (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit). Other agents were working on the same machine.

| Check | Command | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Reproduction | `npx tsc -b packages/workbench; npx playwright test e2e/workbench.spec.ts:375 --project=webkit --repeat-each=8 --workers=4` before the fix | 2 of 8 failed with CI run 6's error: `page.evaluate: Execution context was destroyed, most likely because of a navigation`, thrown from the `expect.poll` callback that read `window.marker` while the shell's intended `location.reload()` was in progress. `expect.poll` does not retry a callback that throws. |
| Fix, WebKit | `npx playwright test e2e/workbench.spec.ts:375 --project=webkit --repeat-each=24 --workers=4` | 24 of 24 passed. |
| Spec, three engines | `npx playwright test e2e/workbench.spec.ts --workers=3` | 54 of 54 passed in Chromium, Firefox, and WebKit. A first run with the default worker count had 3 timeouts in other tests (Firefox `:124`, `:205`; WebKit `:86`, `main h1` not found within 5 s); each passed when rerun alone, and the run with 3 workers passed in full. |
| Formatting | `npx prettier --check e2e/workbench.spec.ts` | Pass. |
| Mutants | 2 mutants of `packages/workbench/src/app/shell.ts` (`.tmp/mutate-e2e.mjs`, WebKit, the fixed spec) | 2 of 2 killed: a shell update no longer reloads; every valid update reloads. |

**Change:** the spec waits for the main frame's `load` event (registered before the configuration is rewritten) and then reads `window.marker` once, instead of polling it through `page.evaluate` across the reload. The shell is unchanged: it reloads on a shell update, as documented in workbench.md §2.1.

**Remaining:** confirmation on CI after the owner pushes.
